              char e = s.charAt(state.pos++)\n                switch (e) {\n                    case '\"':  sb.append('\"' as char); break\n                    case '\\\\': sb.append('\\\\' as char);        boolean dec = false\n        while (state.pos < state.len) {\n            char c = s.charAt(state.pos)\n            if (Character.isDigit(c)) { state.pos++ }\n            else if (c == '.' as     return dec ? new BigDecimal(n) : new BigInteger(n)\n    }\n    parseValue = { ->\n        skipWs()\n        char c = peek()\n        if (c == '{' as char) return parseObject()\n        if (c == '[' as char) return parseArray()\n        if (c == '\"' as char) return parseString()\n        if (c == 't' as char) { state.pos += 4; return Boolean.TRUE }\n        if (c == 'f' as char) { state.pos += 5; return Boolean.FALSE }\n        if (c == 'n' as char) { state.pos += 4; return null }\n        return parseNumber()\n    }\n    skipWs()\n    return parseValue()\n}\n\n// ---- helpers ----\ndef .replaceAll(/`{1,3}(.+?)`{1,3}/, '$1')\n        .trim()\n}\n\ndef htmlToText = { String html ->\n    if (!html) return \"\"\n    return html\n        .replaceAll(/(?i)<br\\s*\\/?>/, \"\\n\")\n    }\n    walk(node)\n    return parts.unique().join(\"\\n\")\n}\n\ndef isPollCard = { c ->\n    if (!(c instanceof Map)) return false\n    if (c?.actions instanceof List) {\n        for (act in
